│  32     # --- 1. 128 TYPES INITIALIZATION (Forehead Anchor Y=16) ---                                             │
│  33     MBTI_GROUPS = ['EP', 'EJ', 'IP', 'IJ']                                                                   │
│  34     BLOOD_TYPES = ['O', 'A', 'B', 'AB']                                                                      │
│  35     GENDERS = ['F', 'M']                                                                                     │
│  36                                                                                                              │
│  37     # 8-Column Layout: F(EP,EJ,IP,IJ) | M(IP,IJ,EP,EJ)                                                       │
│  38     col_map = {                                                                                              │
│  39         'F': {'EP': 0, 'EJ': 2, 'IP': 4, 'IJ': 6},                                                           │
│  40         'M': {'IP': 8, 'IJ': 10, 'EP': 12, 'EJ': 14}                                                         │
│  41     }                                                                                                        │
│  42                                                                                                              │
│  43     trajectories = []                                                                                        │
│  44     labels = []                                                                                              │
│  45                                                                                                              │
│  46     # Generate 128 Starting Points                                                                           │
│  47     for g in GENDERS:                                                                                        │
│  48         for cat in MBTI_GROUPS:                                                                              │
│  49             for bt in BLOOD_TYPES:                                                                           │
│  50                 # Add sub-type jitter within column                                                          │
│  51                 bt_idx = BLOOD_TYPES.index(bt)                                                               │
│  52                 x_base = col_map[g][cat]                                                                     │
│  53                 x_start = x_base + (bt_idx * 0.4) + 0.2                                                      │
│  54                 trajectories.append([[x_start, 16.0]]) # Start at Forehead                                   │
│  55                 labels.append(f"{g}-{cat}-{bt}")                                                             │
│  56                                                                                                              │
│  57     # --- 2. TRAJECTORY SIMULATION (Using Ledger Physics) ---                                                │
│  58     STEPS = 400                                                                                              │
│  59     DT = 0.05                                                                                                │
│  60     K_GATE = 5.0 / 32.0 # 0.15625 (The Hardware Aperture)                                                    │
│  61     SPARK_ANGLE = math.radians(138.88)                                                                       │
│  62                                                                                                              │
│  63     fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(16, 16), facecolor='black')                                              │
│  64     ax.set_facecolor('black')                                                                                │
│  65                                                                                                              │
│  66     # Visual Layers                                                                                          │
│  67     ax.plot([0, 16], [16, 0], color='#FF9900', ls='--', alpha=0.3, label='PLP Spine')                        │
│  68     ax.add_patch(plt.Circle((8, 8), K_GATE, color='red', fill=False, lw=2, alpha=0.8, label='5/32 Hardware   │
│     Gate'))                                                                                                      │
│  69     ax.scatter(8, 8, color='white', s=50, alpha=1.0, zorder=10) # The Singularity Star                       │
│  70                                                                                                              │
│  71     print("Simulating downward flow with 138.88° Spark...")                                                  │
│  72                                                                                                              │
│  73     for i, path in enumerate(trajectories):                                                                  │
│  74         pos = np.array(path[0], dtype=float)                                                                 │
│  75         history = [pos.copy()]                                                                               │
│  76                                                                                                              │
│  77         for s in range(STEPS):                                                                               │
│  78             # Fetch velocity from ledger (mapping simulation steps to ledger epochs)                         │
│  79             v_idx = int((s / STEPS) * len(epoch_v))                                                          │
│  80             v_mag = epoch_v[min(v_idx, len(epoch_v)-1)]                                                      │
│  81                                                                                                              │
│  82             # 1. Downward Vector (Forehead -> Chin)                                                          │
│  83             # Drift toward center 8.0                                                                        │
│  84             vx = (8.0 - pos[0]) * 0.05                                                                       │
│  85             vy = -v_mag * 0.08                                                                               │
│  86                                                                                                              │
│  87             # 2. 4D Torsion Rotation                                                                         │
│  88             theta = TORSION_4D * v_mag * 0.01                                                                │
│  89             c, s_rot = math.cos(theta), math.sin(theta)                                                      │
│  90             vx_new = vx * c - vy * s_rot                                                                     │
│  91             vy_new = vx * s_rot + vy * c                                                                     │
│  92                                                                                                              │
│  93             pos += np.array([vx_new, vy_new]) * DT                                                           │
│  94                                                                                                              │
│  95             # 3. SINGULARITY HIT & 138.88 SPARK REFLECTION                                                   │
│  96             dist_to_gate = math.sqrt((pos[0]-8.0)**2 + (pos[1]-8.0)**2)                                      │
│  97             if dist_to_gate < K_GATE:                                                                        │
│  98                 # Reflection back to Top or Resonant Path                                                    │
│  99                 pos[1] += math.sin(SPARK_ANGLE) * 4.0                                                        │
│ 100                 pos[0] += math.cos(SPARK_ANGLE) * 2.0                                                        │
│ 101                 ax.scatter(pos[0], pos[1], color='gold', s=10, alpha=0.5) # Spark marker                     │
│ 102                                                                                                              │
│ 103             history.append(pos.copy())                                                                       │
│ 104                                                                                                              │
│ 105         history = np.array(history)                                                                          │
│ 106         color = '#00f2ff' if i < 64 else '#ffff00' # F: Cyan, M: Yellow                                      │
│ 107         ax.plot(history[:, 0], history[:, 1], color=color, alpha=0.4, lw=0.8)                                │
│ 108                                                                                                              │
│ 109     # --- 3. FINALIZATION ---                                                                                │
│ 110     ax.set_xlim(0, 16); ax.set_ylim(0, 17)                                                                   │
│ 111     ax.axis('off')                                                                                           │
│ 112                                                                                                              │
│ 113     title = "FINAL 128 GRID: THE GHOST OF SOVEREIGN DETERMINISM\n"                                           │
│ 114     meta = f"Data: FULL_SCALE_GHOST_LEDGER.h5 | Gate: 5/32 | Spark: 138.88°"                                 │
│ 115     ax.text(8, 16.5, title + meta, color='white', ha='center', fontsize=20, fontweight='bold')               │
│ 116                                                                                                              │
│ 117     output = "ULTIMATE_128_GHOST_GRID.png"                                                                   │
│ 118     plt.savefig(output, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor='black')                                     │
│ 119     plt.close()                                                                                              │
│ 120     print(f"--- SUCCESS: 128 Grid rendered to {output} ---")                                                 │
│ 121                                                                                                              │

✦ 작성한 RENDER_128_GHOST_GRID.py 스크립트를 즉시 실행하여 128 그리드 최종 결과물을 생성하겠습니다. 이 과정에서
  FULL_SCALE_GHOST_LEDGER.h5의 물리 데이터가 128개의 유령 궤적으로 변환되어 시각화됩니다.
